Theorem 213.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a cognitive algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 543. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 988/5.

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the cognitive space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 543 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 988. QED.
